- **sync-todo-mcp**: Use sync_todo's own /mcp server -- the Telegram-todo-CalDAV bot's admin-token-gated MCP server exposing todos/settings/calendar-account tools. Also use it to triage or grade new todos, where `scripts/sync_todos.py` and the project's `ai/todos.md` come before `list_todos`. ([luckydonald/archive_apps](https://github.com/luckydonald/archive_apps/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sync-todo-mcp.md) · 2 copies)
- **sync-todos**: This command will synchronize the `todos.md` file with the current state of tasks. ([ranjanpoudel1234/ai-tools](https://github.com/ranjanpoudel1234/ai-tools/blob/HEAD/ClaudeCode/configuration/commands/sync-todos.md) · ★1)
- **sync-todos-to-github**: Convert TodoWrite task list to GitHub plan issue (triggers automation) ([alirezarezvani/claude-code-skill-factory](https://github.com/alirezarezvani/claude-code-skill-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sync-todos-to-github.md) · ★906 · 6 copies)
- **sync-tokens**: Pull the latest design tokens from Figma and regenerate src/theme/ ([anicca-labs/claude](https://github.com/anicca-labs/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/expo-rn-plugin/templates/.claude/commands/sync-tokens.md))
- **sync-toli**: Update the toli repo on this machine (or every machine with `--all`) ([yuyudhan/toli](https://github.com/yuyudhan/toli/blob/HEAD/.omp/commands/sync-toli.md) · ★2)
- **sync-tool-count**: Recount the tools in src/tools and update the counts in the docs ([samaxbytez/freeagent-mcp](https://github.com/samaxbytez/freeagent-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sync-tool-count.md) · ★6)
- **sync-toolbox**: Lift this `.claude/` toolbox into another project. Wraps scripts/sync-toolbox.sh. Prompts for stack selection so only matching skills/hooks/commands land in the target. ([tk-kamyk/kam-score](https://github.com/tk-kamyk/kam-score/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sync-toolbox.md))
